# Documentation Traceability Matrix

Audit date: 13 September 2026. 288 assessment rows; 165 explicit document-namespaced requirement IDs indexed separately. Repeated source requirements share assessments. Status counts are assessment counts, not a percentage of product completion.

Every evidence cell has module, actual API family, table/migration, service/state machine, authorization and test-source information. Table declarations are not proof of deployed persistence. Related tests are not automatically tests of the missing guarantee. Tests were inspected, not executed in this read-only audit. `MISSING` for external approval/pilot evidence means not present in the supplied evidence, not proof that the activity never occurred.
